# ==========================================================================
# 旅遊手冊專案原始碼單一備份檔 (Version: V5.0 - Web Publish & Passcode Release)
# ==========================================================================
# 此文字檔整合了 index.html, styles.css, auth.js, sw.js 與 app.js 的所有代碼。
# 方便您在沒有原檔案的情況下，直接複製這份文字檔的內容在其他電腦還原。
#
# 注意：此備份檔「不包含」任何真實行程資料。
#       真實行程只存在於瀏覽器的 localStorage，以及 data/ 資料夾裡
#       加密過的行程檔（需要密碼才能解開）。
# ==========================================================================

:root {
    /* 色彩系統 - 日系暖沙與陶橘 */
    --bg-screen: #F6F2EB;
    --bg-card: #FFFFFF;
    --primary: #C85A32;       /* 陶土橘 - 溫暖日系 */
    --primary-light: #FCEFE9;
    --secondary: #2B4C59;     /* 靛石藍 - 質感穩重 */
    --secondary-light: #EBF1F3;
    --accent: #DFAB4E;        /* 暖陽金 - 重點標示 */
    --text-main: #1C1C1C;     /* 深炭灰 - 護眼高對比 */
    --text-muted: #5E5E5E;
    --border-color: #E2DDD5;
    --success: #5F8D58;
    --success-light: #EEF5ED;
    --warning: #D89047;
    --warning-light: #FEF6EC;
    
    /* 標楷體字型系統 (Mac: BiauKai, Windows: DFKai-SB, 標楷體) */
    --font-kai: "BiauKai", "DFKai-SB", "標楷體", "Noto Sans TC", serif;
    --font-sans: "Outfit", "Noto Sans TC", sans-serif;
    --radius-lg: 12px;
    --radius-md: 8px;
    --radius-sm: 4px;
    --transition: all 0.2s ease-in-out;
    --shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(44, 44, 44, 0.05);
    --shadow-hover: 0 8px 25px rgba(44, 44, 44, 0.1);

    /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------
       風格主題變數 (Theme layer)
       ----------------------------------------------------------------------
       這些變數控制「風格」下拉選單切換的視覺差異。:root 這組預設值 = 日系
       手帳風，所以沒套用任何 style-* class 時外觀跟以前完全一樣。
       body.style-XXX 只覆寫這些變數（必要時再加少量微調），版面骨架仍由
       「排版版型」template-XXX 決定 —— 兩者互不干擾，可自由組合。
       ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
    --paper-grad: linear-gradient(135deg, #FAF6F0 0%, #EFE7DC 100%);
    --paper-dot: rgba(43, 76, 89, 0.06);        /* 紙張點陣紋理 (螢幕) */
    --paper-dot-faint: rgba(43, 76, 89, 0.04);  /* 紙張點陣紋理 (列印/四折) */
    --panel-bg: #FFFFFF;                        /* 內容面板底色 */
    --tape-color: rgba(200, 90, 50, 0.25);      /* 紙膠帶 */
    --tape-display: block;                      /* 紙膠帶顯示與否 */
    --photo-frame-bg: #FFFFFF;                  /* 拍立得白框底色 */
    --photo-frame-border: 1px solid #D2C9B9;
    --photo-frame-pad: 8px 8px 20px 8px;        /* 下緣較厚 = 拍立得 */
    --title-family: var(--font-kai);            /* 大標字體 */
    --title-spacing: normal;
    --title-weight: bold;
}

           at once re-triggers the base .book-page fadeIn animation for every
           non-active page simultaneously, causing a visible blank/faded flash
           across the whole booklet when preview mode is switched on. */
        animation: none !important;
        opacity: 1 !important;
        transform: none !important;

    body.booklet-preview .cover-layout {
        height: 100% !important;

    }

       each only have 2 direct children inside .day-layout (header + one content
       block). The generic rule above stretches .day-layout to the full 297mm
       page height and uses `justify-content: space-between`, which -- with only
       2 children -- shoves the second block all the way down to the bottom of
       the tall page, leaving a huge empty gap under the header. This is exactly
       what made booklet-preview look "weird" while the print output (which uses
       `justify-content: flex-start` + `height: auto`, see the @media print rule
       for .day-layout below) looked normal. Match print's behavior here so both
       views agree. */
    body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .day-page-detailed-a .day-layout,
    body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .day-page-detailed-b .day-layout {

    }

           (screen) rule. On screen only the active page is display:block, so the
           animation only ever plays for the page currently being switched to.
           But this print block forces EVERY page to display:flex at once, which
           re-triggers that fade-in animation on all of them simultaneously. If the
           browser/PDF engine renders before the 0.2s finishes, every page except
           the one already visible on screen is captured mid-fade (or fully
           transparent) -- printing/exporting as blank pages. Force the animation

/* ==========================================================================
   V4.0 排版版型切換樣式 (Template Layout Styles)
   ========================================================================== */

/* 1. 雙頁詳細版 (template-detailed) */

/* FIX: the generic `body.booklet-preview .content { max-width: 800px }` rule
   further down was sized for the single A4 panel templates (compact /
   text-heavy). For template-detailed the two A5 panels need ~1140px to sit
   side by side (561px x 2 + gap) — inside an 800px column they could never
   fit next to each other and always wrapped onto separate rows, which is
   the "上下" stacking the left-right pairing fix above could not solve by
   itself. Widen the column specifically for this template. */
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .content {
    max-width: 1260px !important;
}

/* The paired A5 spread needs ~1140px to sit side by side; on a browser
   window narrower than that it would be silently clipped by the base
   `body { overflow-x: hidden }` rule instead of just scrolling into view.
   Let this specific view scroll horizontally so nothing gets lost. */
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview {
    overflow-x: auto !important;
}

/* Booklet Preview Mode on Screen */
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .book-pages {
    display: flex !important;
    flex-wrap: wrap !important;
    gap: 15px !important;
    justify-content: center !important;
    background-color: #525659 !important;
    padding: 20px !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    box-sizing: border-box !important;
}

/* FIX: the Day1-5 pages are injected into a plain <div id="dynamic-day-pages">
   wrapper (see index.html), which sits between .book-pages (the flex
   container) and the individual .book-page sections. A plain block div
   is not a flex item's transparent pass-through, so the day pages fell
   out of the flex-wrap pairing and stacked top-to-bottom instead of
   pairing left-right like every other spread. display:contents makes the
   wrapper invisible to layout so its children become direct flex items
   of .book-pages again, restoring the left-right A/B pairing on screen
   (print already worked because it uses float instead of flex). */
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview #dynamic-day-pages {
    display: contents !important;
}

body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .book-page {
    display: block !important;
    box-sizing: border-box !important;
    background-color: #FFFFFF !important;
    box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3) !important;
    margin-bottom: 20px !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    width: 148.5mm !important; /* Default A5 size panels */
    height: 210mm !important;
    min-height: 210mm !important;
    max-height: 210mm !important;
    padding: 10mm 8mm !important;
    border-radius: 0 !important;
    border: none !important;
    position: relative !important;
}

/* Full-width sheets (Cover is now A5, Cards and Packing are full A4 landscape) */
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview #page-cards,
body.template-detailed.booklet-preview #page-packing {
    width: 297mm !important;
    padding: 10mm 15mm !important;
}

body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .day-page-detailed-a {
    border-right: 1px dashed #CCCCCC !important;
}

body.template-detailed.booklet-preview .day-page-detailed-b {
    margin-right: 40px !important; /* Visual page separation */
}

/* Interactive webpage view (not booklet-preview, not print): clicking a day
   in the nav marks BOTH page-dayN-a and page-dayN-b .active at once (see
   switchPage() in app.js), same as preview/print. Without pairing CSS here
   they just stacked as two full-width blocks — the one place that still
   looked different from what preview/print show. Give this view the same
   left-right spread, sized responsively (%) instead of print's fixed mm,
   since this is the page people browse on a laptop or phone while
   traveling, not a paper simulation. */
body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) #dynamic-day-pages {
    display: contents;
}

body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) .book-pages {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: flex-start;
    /* 0 here on purpose: the day-a/day-b pair below butts together with no
       gap so it reads as one card (see comment below). Every other page is
       alone on its row (flex-basis 100%), so this gap never shows for them
       anyway. */
    gap: 0;
}

body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) .book-page.active {
    flex: 1 1 100%;
    width: 100%;
}

/* Every other page (cover / cards / packing / notes) is ONE .book-page
   element carrying ONE border+shadow "paper card" frame, even when its
   content is internally split into two CSS-grid columns (cards, packing).
   The day pages are the odd one out: -a and -b are two SEPARATE .book-page
   elements, so pairing them with a gap produced two distinct bordered
   boxes with a gap of plain background between — a visibly different
   "frame shape" than every other page, which read as the border jumping
   around as you click through the nav. Butt them together with no gap and
   only round/shadow the outer edges so the pair reads as the same single
   card-with-a-divider language as cards/packing, instead of two cards. */
body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) .day-page-detailed-a.active,
body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) .day-page-detailed-b.active {
    flex: 1 1 50%;
    width: 50%;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow) !important;
}

body.template-detailed:not(.booklet-preview) .day-page-detailed-a.active {
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg) 0 0 var(--radius-lg) !important;
    border-right: none !important;
}

/* ==========================================================================
   COMPACT TEMPLATE (單頁經典版) - 每日行程改為真正的「四象限」排版：
   左上滿版圖片 / 右上今日交通 / 左下漫遊小貼士 / 右下景點簡介。
   只套用在 template-compact，不影響 detailed / text-heavy / fourfold 既有排版。
   做法：不改動 app.js 產出的 HTML 結構（避免牽動其他版型），改用
   display:contents 把 .day-top-grid / .day-sidebar / .day-bottom-timeline
   這三層包裝「攤平」，讓裡面 4 個真正的內容區塊（照片、交通卡、小貼士卡、
   景點簡介）直接成為 .day-layout 這個 grid 的格子，各自指定 grid-area。
   ========================================================================== */
body.template-compact .day-top-grid,
body.template-compact .day-sidebar,
body.template-compact .day-bottom-timeline {
    display: contents !important;
}

body.template-compact .day-layout {
    display: grid !important;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.1fr !important;
    grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr !important;
    grid-template-areas:
        "header     header"
        "image      transport"
        "tips       timeline"
        "gallery    gallery"
        "meals      meals" !important;
    gap: 8px 18px !important;
}

/* 照片牆與餐食表都要橫跨整個版面寬度，否則會被自動塞進左欄變成細長一條 */
body.template-compact .day-gallery { grid-area: gallery !important; }
body.template-compact .day-meals   { grid-area: meals !important; }

body.template-compact .day-header { grid-area: header !important; }

body.template-compact .day-hero-image {
    grid-area: image !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    max-width: 100% !important;
    height: 100% !important;
    max-height: none !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
}

body.template-compact .day-hero-image .day-art {
    width: 100% !important;
    height: 100% !important;
    max-height: none !important;
    object-fit: cover !important;
}

/* .day-sidebar has 1-2 .info-card children: the plain one is 今日交通與說明,
   the one with the extra .warning class is 漫遊小貼士. */
body.template-compact .day-top-grid .info-card:not(.warning) { grid-area: transport !important; }
body.template-compact .day-top-grid .info-card.warning { grid-area: tips !important; }

body.template-compact .day-timeline {
    grid-area: timeline !important;
    padding-left: 0 !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
}

/* On phone-width screens a 2-column quadrant grid leaves each cell too
   narrow to read comfortably -- stack all four blocks in one column instead,
   which is exactly the layout someone actually wants while scrolling on a
   phone during the trip. */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
    body.template-compact .day-layout {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;
        grid-template-rows: auto !important;
        grid-template-areas:
            "header"
            "image"
            "transport"
            "tips"
            "timeline"
            "gallery"
            "meals" !important;
    }
    body.template-compact .day-gallery { grid-area: gallery !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-meals   { grid-area: meals !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-hero-image {
        height: auto !important;
        max-height: 260px !important;
    }
    body.template-compact .day-hero-image .day-art {
        height: auto !important;
        max-height: 260px !important;
    }
}

@media print {
    body.template-compact .day-layout {
        display: grid !important;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.1fr !important;
        /* 照片牆與餐食表各自一列，且都橫跨兩欄。少了這兩列的話它們會被自動
           placement 丟進同一格互相疊在一起，並把整個版面推出紙外。 */
        grid-template-rows: auto 1fr 1fr auto auto !important;
        grid-template-areas:
            "header     header"
            "image      transport"
            "tips       timeline"
            "gallery    gallery"
            "meals      meals" !important;
        gap: 6mm 6mm !important;
        height: 100% !important;
    }
    body.template-compact .day-gallery { grid-area: gallery !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-meals   { grid-area: meals !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-header { grid-area: header !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-hero-image {
        grid-area: image !important;
        width: 100% !important;
        max-width: 100% !important;
        height: 100% !important;
        max-height: none !important;
        margin: 0 !important;
    }
    body.template-compact .day-hero-image .day-art {
        width: 100% !important;
        height: 100% !important;
        max-height: none !important;
        object-fit: cover !important;
    }
    body.template-compact .day-top-grid .info-card:not(.warning) { grid-area: transport !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-top-grid .info-card.warning { grid-area: tips !important; }
    body.template-compact .day-timeline {
        grid-area: timeline !important;
        padding-left: 0 !important;
    }

/* ==========================================================================
   風格主題 (VISUAL STYLE THEMES)
   ==========================================================================
   「風格」= 配色 / 字體 / 邊框 / 裝飾 (這一段)
   「排版版型」= 版面骨架 template-XXX (上面各段)
   兩者是獨立的兩軸，任何風格都能套用在任何版型上。

   實作方式刻意以覆寫 :root 變數為主、結構調整為輔 —— 這樣新增風格時只要
   加一組變數，不必再把每個版型 × 每個顯示情境重寫一次。
   ========================================================================== */

/* ==========================================================================
   旅遊資訊頁 (Info pages)
   ==========================================================================
   注意事項 / 搭機資訊 / 飯店資訊 / 旅遊資訊。內容量大，由 app.js 的分頁引擎
   量測後切成多頁，所以這裡只負責「一頁之內」的樣式，不處理換頁。
   所有顏色與字體都走主題變數，四種風格自動適用。
   ========================================================================== */

/* 量測盒：離開畫面、不可見、不參與互動，只用來量區塊高度。
   寬度必須等同該版型「一欄」的實際寬度，量出來的高度才準。 */
.info-measure-box {
    position: absolute;
    left: -99999px;
    top: 0;
    visibility: hidden;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: -1;
}

/* 各版型「一欄」的實際內容寬度：
   單頁版 = A4 直式 210mm 扣掉 @page 左右各 15mm

/* 四折頁口袋版：每個摺頁面板只有 74mm × 210mm，實測加入餐食表後每日行程頁
   會超出紙張 40～109px（最寬鬆的一頁也只剩 17px 餘裕），放不下。
   與消費記錄表採相同作法：螢幕上照常可以填寫，預覽與列印則不放這張表，
   讓「預覽 = 印出來」維持成立，不會有半張表被紙緣裁掉。 */
body.template-fourfold.booklet-preview .day-meals {
    display: none !important;
}

@media print {
    body.template-fourfold .day-meals {
        display: none !important;
    }
}

/* 雜誌編輯風的放大標題是為了 A4 整頁的視覺衝擊，套到四折頁那種 74mm 寬的
   窄摺頁上會過大，把內容擠出紙外（實測 page-day3-a 溢出 10px）。
   四折頁改回接近一般級數。 */
body.style-magazine.template-fourfold .page-title { font-size: 15px !important; }
body.style-magazine.template-fourfold .day-title  { font-size: 13px !important; }


/* ==========================================================================
   簽名排版模組 (Signature layout modules)
   ==========================================================================
   同一份 HTML 結構，靠風格切換出完全不同的排法 —— 這是「換風格像換了一本
   手冊」的關鍵。三個模組：今日推薦照片牆、車程時間軸、側邊分類頁籤。
   ========================================================================== */

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   模組 A：今日推薦照片牆（基準樣式 = 方格）
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.day-gallery {
    margin-top: 10px;
}

.day-gallery-title {
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: bold;
    color: var(--secondary);
    font-family: var(--title-family);
    margin-bottom: 6px;
}

/* auto-fill + minmax：格子大小由「最小寬度」決定，欄數自己算。
   這樣同一組 CSS 在 A4 整頁（很寬）與 A5 半張（很窄）都會給出合理的格子
   尺寸，不會像固定 3 欄那樣在寬頁面上把格子撐成巨無霸吃掉整頁。 */
.day-gallery-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(74px, 1fr));
    gap: 8px;
}

.gallery-tile-img {
    max-height: 26mm;
}

.gallery-tile {
    position: relative;
    margin: 0;
    text-align: center;
}

.gallery-tile-img {
    width: 100%;
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    background-color: var(--panel-bg);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: var(--transition);
}

.gallery-tile-img:not(.has-img)::after {
    content: "📷";
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    height: 100%;
    font-size: 18px;
    opacity: 0.35;
}

.gallery-tile-img:hover { filter: brightness(0.93); }

.gallery-tile figcaption {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1px;
    margin-top: 3px;
    line-height: 1.35;
}

.gallery-tile-name {
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: bold;
    color: var(--text-main);
}

.gallery-tile-note {
    font-size: 8.5px;

/* ==========================================================================
   拼版模式 (Saddle-stitch imposition)
   ==========================================================================
   雙面列印 → 對折 → 騎馬釘。每一張橫式 A4 對折後產生兩個 A5 頁面，
   所以小冊子模式下「每一頁都必須是獨立的 A5」，不能有跨過摺線的整張版面。
   ========================================================================== */

@media print {
    /* 所有動態頁的 wrapper 都要對版面透明，拼版才能把全部頁面排進同一個
       flex 容器裡依 order 擺位。 */
    body.imposition #dynamic-day-pages,
    body.imposition #dynamic-info-pages,
    body.imposition #dynamic-custom-pages {
        display: contents !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition .book-pages {
        display: flex !important;
        flex-wrap: wrap !important;
        align-content: flex-start !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition .book-page {
        float: none !important;
        flex: 0 0 148.5mm !important;
    }

    /* 刷卡攻略與行李清單原本是「整張橫式紙、內容跨過摺線」的設計，
       在小冊子裡必須收成單一 A5 頁面，內部改單欄。 */
    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-cards,
    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing {
        display: block !important;
        width: 148.5mm !important;
        padding: 10mm 8mm !important;
        page-break-before: auto !important;
        page-break-after: auto !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-cards::before,
    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing::before {
        display: none !important;   /* 單頁了，不需要中央摺線 */
    }

    /* 刷卡攻略的兩張卡片盒需要寬度，單欄；行李清單四類單欄會超出 A5
       （實測 +85～100px），改雙欄剛好塞得下。 */
    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-cards .cards-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;
        gap: 6px !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing .packing-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr !important;
        gap: 5px !important;
        align-items: start !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing .packing-category {
        padding: 6px 8px !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing .check-item {
        font-size: 8.5px !important;
        line-height: 1.4 !important;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-cards .card-notes-printable,
    body.template-detailed.imposition #page-packing .flight-lodging-section {
        display: none !important;   /* A5 放不下，且各自已有專屬頁面 */
    }

    /* 補白頁：維持紙張底色，不留突兀的白塊 */
    body.imposition .impose-blank {
        background:
            radial-gradient(var(--paper-dot-faint) 1.2px, transparent 1.2px),
            var(--paper-grad) !important;
        background-size: 16px 16px, 100% 100% !important;
    }

    /* 摺線記號：只在紙張上下邊緣打短記號，不貫穿內容。
       落在每個 A5 頁面的外側邊界 = 對折線的位置。 */
    body.template-detailed.imposition .impose-right::before,
    body.template-detailed.imposition .impose-right::after {
        content: "";
        position: absolute;
        left: 0;
        width: 0;
        border-left: 0.4mm dashed #9A9A9A;
        height: 5mm;
    }

    body.template-detailed.imposition .impose-right::before { top: 0; }
    body.template-detailed.imposition .impose-right::after  { bottom: 0; }

    body.template-detailed.imposition .book-page {
        position: relative;
    }
}
